  <section
    id="od-locpg-how-it-works"
    class="od_locpg_hiworks_section od-locpg-presto-container"
  >
    <div class="od-locpg-hiwroks-header">
      <h2 id="i97c98" class="od_locpg_hiworks_heading">
        How to Reserve Your
        <span
          presto-non-editable="true"
          title="Not Editable"
          contenteditable="false"
          class="LocationName"
          >{{.LocationName}}</span
        >
        Vacation Rental
      </h2>
      <h4 class="od_locpg_hiworks_subheading">Booking Made Simple</h4>
      <p class="od_locpg_hiworks_desc">
        Owner Direct helps travelers find verified vacation rentals from trusted
        sources. Once you select a property you love, you’ll be guided to our
        partner’s secure booking platform to complete your reservation.
